mechanism**.\r\n\r\nFacebook is **NOT a dashboard-access requirement**.\r\n\r\nFacebook

Knowledge\n\n### Staff App — User Model (`staff/models.py`)\n- Custom 
`User(AbstractUser)` with fields: `phone`, `bio`, `photo`, `gender`, social URLs 
(facebook, twitter, instagram, tiktok — all **optional**)\n- `facebook_verified` field 
EXISTS on model but is a `BooleanField(default=False)` — NOT used for auth gating\n- 
`ROLE_CHOICES`: `GENERAL`, `STATE`, `ZONAL`, `LGA`, `WARD`\n- `STATUS_CHOICES`: 
`PENDING`, `UNDER_REVIEW`, `VERIFIED`, `REJECTED`, `SUSPENDED` ← **NOT** 
`ACTIVE/INACTIVE` — these are the exact 5 statuses\n- `REPORTER_LEVEL_CHOICES`: 
`MEMBER`, `COMMUNITY_REPORTER`, `TRUSTED_REPORTER`\n- `is_trusted_reporter` = 
BooleanField, granted by President or Director of Media\n- `role_definition` = FK to 
`leadership.RoleDefinition` (nullable)\n- `can_approve_members()` returns True for 
`STATE`, `ZONAL`, `LGA` with `VERIFIED` status\n- `get_jurisdiction()` → 
`STATE`=state-wide, `ZONAL`=zone.name, `LGA`=lga.name, `WARD`=ward.name\n\n### Core App 
— Key Models (`core/models.py`)\n- **`Report`** model: `WARD_TO_LGA`, `LGA_TO_ZONAL`, 
`ZONAL_TO_STATE` types; statuses: `DRAFT`, `SUBMITTED`, `UNDER_REVIEW`, `APPROVED`, 
`FLAGGED`, `REJECTED`, `ESCALATED`; has `parent_report` FK (hierarchical chain); 
`can_be_escalated()` only if APPROVED + not yet escalated + WARD_TO_LGA or LGA_TO_ZONAL 
type\n- **`CommunityReport`** (public \"Report a Story\"): statuses `PENDING`, 
`UNDER_REVIEW`, `ESCALATED`, `APPROVED`, `REJECTED`; `INFO_STATUS_CHOICES`: `VERIFIED`, 
`CONFIRMED`, `DEVELOPING`, `ALERT`; has `evidence_image` and `evidence_video` upload 
fields\n- **`Announcement`**: scopes `GENERAL`, `ZONAL`, `LGA`, `WARD`; priorities 
`LOW`, `NORMAL`, `HIGH`, `URGENT`; has `Announcement.for_user(user)` classmethod that 
filters by user's zone/lga/ward\n- **`Notification`** model EXISTS: `INFO`, `SUCCESS`, 
`WARNING`, `ACTION` types; per-user, with `is_read`, `link`, `title`, `message` — this 
IS a real notification system\n- **`Opportunity`** categories: `SCHOLARSHIP`, `GRANT`, 
`JOB`, `INTERNSHIP`, `VOLUNTEER`, `ENTREPRENEURSHIP`, `TRAINING`, `EMPOWERMENT`, 
`FELLOWSHIP`; statuses: `OPEN`, `CLOSING_SOON`, `CLOSED`\n- **`Patron`**: `GRAND` or 
`PATRON` type; `full_name`, `title`, `bio`, `photo`, `is_published`, `order`\n\n### 
Staff App — Programs\n- **`WomensProgram`**, **`YouthProgram`**, **`WelfareProgram`** 
all have same pattern: title, description, program_type, status 
(PLANNED/ONGOING/COMPLETED/CANCELLED), zone/lga FKs (scope), start_date/end_date, 
participants M2M, budget, created_by\n- **`DisciplinaryAction`**: `WARNING`, 
`SUSPENSION`, `DISMISSAL`; requires `legal_reviewed_by` + `legal_approved` (Legal 
Adviser role involved)\n- **`WardMeeting`** / **`WardMeetingAttendance`**: ward-level 
meetings with attendance tracking through table\n- **`CommunityOutreach`**: engagement 
type, status, organization contact, follow-up system\n\n### Campaigns App — 
Article/Newsroom (`campaigns/models.py`)\n- Model named `Campaign` but 
`verbose_name='Article'`\n- `content_json` = JSONField using **Editor.js block format** 
— Android needs a native block content renderer (NO WebView)\n- 
`VERIFICATION_STATUS_CHOICES`: `DEVELOPING`, `CONFIRMED`, `VERIFIED`, 
`COMMUNITY_ALERT`\n- `REPORTER_CREDIT_CHOICES`: `KPN_REPORTER`, `KPN_CORRESPONDENT`, 
`KPN_LGA`, `KPN_STATE`, `KPN_EDITORIAL`\n- Status workflow: `DRAFT` → `PENDING` → 
`PUBLISHED` / `REJECTED` / `ARCHIVED`\n- `get_read_time()` computes from block content 
word count\n- Has `lga` and `ward` FK for geographic tagging\n\n### Telegram 
Integration (`telegram_integration/models.py`)\n- **`TelegramMembership`** (OneToOne 
with User): `telegram_user_id` (BigInt, permanent), `telegram_username` (may change), 
`telegram_status` choices: `creator`, `administrator`, `member`, `restricted`, `left`, 
`kicked`, `not_member`\n- `is_verified` = True only when confirmed active channel 
member\n- `is_active_member()` = `is_verified AND telegram_status in (creator, 
administrator, member)`\n- `mark_left()` sets `is_verified=False`, `left_at=now`, 
`telegram_status='left'`\n- Timestamps: `first_verified_at`, `last_verified_at`, 
`last_checked_at`, `left_at`\n- **`KPNMeeting`**: types `STATE_EXECUTIVE`, `ZONAL`, 
`LGA`, `WARD`, `SPECIAL`, `GENERAL`; statuses `SCHEDULED`, `ACTIVE`, `ENDED`, 
`CANCELLED`; has `telegram_message_id` for channel attendance announcement\n- 
**`MeetingAttendance`**: `/present` bot command records `confirmed_at`; statuses 
`PRESENT`, `LATE`, `ABSENT`, `PARTIAL` — attendance is confirmation-time based, NOT 
voice-chat presence\n- **`TelegramAuthState`**: CSRF/state tokens for Telegram Login 
Widget — prevents replay attacks\n- **`AIRateLimit`**: per-Telegram-user AI request 
rate limiting (daily + hourly)\n- **`AIProviderConfig`**: DB-driven AI provider 
switching — supports `openrouter`, `openai_compatible`, `google` protocols — API keys 
stored server-side in DB\n\n### Existing REST API (confirmed from 
`rest_api/urls.py`)\n```\nGET  /api/v1/newsroom/\nGET  /api/v1/newsroom/<slug>/\nGET  
/api/v1/opportunities/\nGET  /api/v1/community/\nGET  /api/v1/civic/\nGET  
/api/v1/advocacy/\nGET  /api/v1/leadership-directory/\nGET  /api/v1/patrons/\nGET  
/api/v1/impact/\nGET  /api/v1/locations/zones/\nGET  /api/v1/locations/lgas/\nGET  
/api/v1/locations/wards/\nGET  /api/v1/roles/\nPOST /api/v1/auth/register/\nPOST 
/api/v1/auth/login/      (JWT via SimpleJWT)\nPOST /api/v1/auth/refresh/\nGET  
/api/v1/auth/me/\nGET  /api/v1/schema/\nGET  /api/v1/docs/            (Swagger UI via 
drf-spectacular)\n```\n\n**Confirmed API gaps** (no REST endpoints exist for): 
dashboards, member management, approvals, announcements, notifications, ward meetings, 
KPN meetings, reports, disciplinary actions, programs (women/youth/welfare), community

Staff App — Views & Decorators (`staff/decorators.py`, `staff/views.py`)\n- 
`specific_role_required` decorator hardcodes \"Super Access\" for `President` and 
`Director of Media & Communications`.\n- The `dashboard` view routes users to 1 of 40 
specific dashboard templates based on their `RoleDefinition.title`.\n- Registration 
requires a `RoleDefinition` ID; the `GENERAL` role is disabled for direct registration 
(requires assignment).\n- ID Card generation (`generate_id_tag`) uses `PIL` and 
`reportlab`. It restricts `GENERAL` members.\n- Disciplinary actions have a workflow: 
created by any approved leader, must be legally reviewed by `Legal & Ethics Adviser`, 
then approved by `President` or State Executive.\n- Trust levels: `COMMUNITY_REPORTER` 
and `TRUSTED_REPORTER` are managed exclusively by President/Media Director.\n- Program 
management (WomensProgram, YouthProgram, WelfareProgram) uses jurisdiction filtering 
(`zone`, `lga`, `ward`) to prevent IDOR — leaders can only manage programs and 
participants in their jurisdiction.\n\n### REST API (`rest_api/views.py`, 
`rest_api/serializers.py`)\n- The existing REST API is **extremely limited**. It only 
covers public data (Newsroom, Opportunities, Impact, Patrons, Locations, Roles) and 
basic JWT Auth (`register`, `login`, `me`).\n- **GAPS IDENTIFIED FOR ANDROID**: There 
are **zero** endpoints for internal operations. To support the native Android app, the 
backend will need massive API extensions for: Dashboards, Announcements, Programs, 
Disciplinary Actions, Meetings, Donations, Community Reports, Approvals, Member 
Management, and ID Card generation.\n\n### Donations & Events Models 
(`donations/models.py`, `events/models.py`)\n- `Donation` has statuses `UNVERIFIED`, 
`VERIFIED`, `RECORDED`.\n- `AuditReport` has a workflow: `DRAFT` (Auditor General) -> 
`SUBMITTED` (to President) -> `REVIEWED`.\n- `MeetingMinutes` are tied 1:1 to an 
`Event`.\n\n### General Architecture\n- **Permissions**: Based on `status` 
(`VERIFIED`), `role` (`STATE`, `ZONAL`, `LGA`, `WARD`, `GENERAL`), and specific 
`RoleDefinition`. Jurisdiction (`zone`, `lga`, `ward`) is strictly enforced in list 
views.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Files and Code\n\n### Scratch Scripts Created\n- `c:\\Users\\hp\\
